# TempReason — 90 selected samples

English draft for human review. Azerbaijani translation has not been performed.
L1 is an exact unchanged selection from the source. L2 and L3 are fictional Azerbaijani localizations, not factual claims about real people, clubs, organizations, or Azerbaijani history.
All original numeric strings, event dates, durations, question direction, answer cardinality and fact_context line order are preserved. Fictional descriptive club names are not claims that those clubs exist.
L1 date calculations were recomputed. L2 answers were checked against inclusive month intervals in fact_context. L3 answers were checked against adjacent fact_context entries sorted by start and end month. These checks use fact_context, not independent real-world verification of biographies.
Some source prose and structured facts disagree, including historical club transition years and later employment. They remain unchanged apart from localization. Use the structured fact_context as the reference for these draft tasks; review which context field your evaluation prompt will expose before final acceptance.
The selection focuses on short sports careers and office-holder timelines and is not a representative random sample. Related profiles recur across L2/L3 to permit comparison; the outputs are not independent training and test splits.
The original source may contain spelling errors, duplicated profile headings and sentence fragments. Those source defects are not silently repaired by the localization.
A newline separates source titles and opening sentences or footer club names that were originally concatenated; their text is retained and localized. Necessary article agreement after substitutions is adjusted.

## Level 2 — 30 samples

### 01. az_tempreason_l2_001

Source position: **229** (1-based).

**Question:** Which team did Namiq Quliyev play for in Oct, 1951?

**Answer:** ["Ganja senior football club"]

**Context:**

Namiq Quliyev
Namiq Quliyev (4 January 1918 – 20 March 2010) was an Azerbaijani football player famous in Azerbaijan in the 1940s and 1950s.Born in Goychay, Azerbaijan, Quliyev was a particularly quick right winger able to run 100 m in 11 seconds. He left his town in Shirvan as a youngster to join his brother in Shaki, making his footballing debut in the domestic top flight at the age of 15. Following the 1939 regional conflict, he was noted by an Azerbaijani sports professor who suggested him to Baku senior football club. As he was considered a local citizen following the regional boundary changes, he was allowed to enrol in the Baku Institute of Physical Education whilst also playing for Baku senior football club, where he won a national title in 1941–42. He played for the "Baku players" until 1948, when he joined Ganja senior football club; he finally retired in 1953.He later started an coaching career, serving also as Baku senior football club head coach for a short time during the 1963–64 season as a replacement for Asif Hacıyev. He then became a scout for Baku senior football club, for which he discovered a young Cavid Məcidov when played in a local club Goychay youth football club.Quliyev died in Baku at the age of 92 in 2010. He was the last living player of the first historical Baku senior football club "league title". Baku senior football club, who was scheduled to play an evening game against Shirvan football club on that day, played the game with black armbands to honour him.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Namiq Quliyev plays for Ganja senior football club from Jan, 1947 to Jan, 1953. 
 Namiq Quliyev plays for Lankaran 1944 football club from Jan, 1953 to Jan, 1954. 
 Namiq Quliyev plays for Shaki central football club from Jan, 1933 to Jan, 1939. 
 Namiq Quliyev plays for Baku senior football club from Jan, 1939 to Jan, 1947.
```

He left his town in Kosovo as a youngster to join his brother in Tirana, making his footballing debut in the domestic top flight at the age of 15. Following the 1939 Italian invasion of Albania, he was noted by an Italian sports professor who suggested him to AS Roma. As he was considered an Italian following the annexation of Albania, he was allowed to enrol in the Rome \"ISEF\" (Italian sports university) whilst also playing for AS Roma, where he won an Italian title in 1941–42. He played for the \"giallorossi\" until 1948, when he joined A.C. ### 02. az_tempreason_l2_002

Source position: **212** (1-based).

**Question:** Which team did Orxan Həmidov play for in Jan, 2001?

**Answer:** ["Lankaran regional under-17 football team"]

**Context:**

Orxan Həmidov
Orxan Həmidov (16 July 1984) is an Azerbaijani retired footballer.Born in Baku, Azerbaijan, Həmidov began his playing career at local football club Lankaran junior football club from Lankaran. He started his professional career playing for regional side Shaki youth football club in 2002. However, he signed for Ganja youth football club in February 2003, teaming up with fellow Azerbaijanis Qabil Məmmədov and Cəlil Qasımov. He made his debut on 28 February, against Guba football club. He was also a runner-up for the Best Young Player award of 2003/2004 season. Həmidov scored once for Ganja youth club in Caspian Cup.In 2004, Həmidov went for trials with regional club Gusar football club in the summer, but eventually joined Northern Regional League side Sumgait senior football club. He signed a four and a half year contract. He joined Lankaran senior football club in 2009 and signed in August 2010 for regional club side Sabirabad football club.Həmidov was a member of Lankarans U-17 squad that finished second in the 2001 National U-17 Championship held in Absheron. He started all six games, and scored twice from his midfield position. Həmidov scored the last goal against Shaki District on a penalty kick in the 91st minute, in which Lankaran team won 4-0. He was also on target in the 5-1 defeat of Shirvan team in the quarter final. Lankaran team eventually lost the final against Absheron team.Həmidov is the nephew of Kamil Həmidov, cousin of Tofiq Həsənov, elder brother of Fərid Həmidov.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Orxan Həmidov plays for Sabirabad football club from Jan, 2010 to Jan, 2011. 
 Orxan Həmidov plays for Lankaran senior football club from Jan, 2009 to Jan, 2010. 
 Orxan Həmidov plays for Guba senior football club from Jan, 2008 to Jan, 2009. 
 Orxan Həmidov plays for Shaki youth football club from Jan, 2002 to Jan, 2002. 
 Orxan Həmidov plays for Ganja youth football club from Jan, 2002 to Jan, 2004. 
 Orxan Həmidov plays for Lankaran regional under-17 football team from Jan, 2001 to Jan, 2001. 
 Orxan Həmidov plays for Sumgait senior football club from Jan, 2004 to Jan, 2006.
```

Davud Nəcəfov
Davud Nəcəfov (born 16 July 1985) is an Azerbaijani footballer who plays for Goychay senior football club as a midfielder. During his career, he played for Goychay, Shamakhi senior football club, Sumgait junior football club, Ganja central football club, Shirvan central football club, Gusar central football club, Guba junior football club, , and Goychay senior football club.Nəcəfov acquired Azerbaijani citizenship on 23 August 2012.Coming from the village of , near Goychay, in the region of Shirvan, Nəcəfov went through the ranks of several lower-tier local clubs, debuting at the age of 15 for the seniors of Goychay junior football club, before joining the U17 team of Goychay football academy. In his last season of eligibility for the U19 team, he left the club, however, and joined the Regional Second Division side Goychay central football club, where he became a fixture for the following several seasons.In 2007, he joined the Regional Premier League side Shamakhi senior football club. He soon established himself in the first team, and, after winning his first league title with the team, in 2009, won the fans' award "Bənövşəyi döyüşçü" (Purple Warrior) awarded to the most distinguished player of the past season. Shamakhi senior football club went on to win four titles in the following five seasons, with Nəcəfov featuring heavily in all campaigns, and was selected for the league's ideal eleven of the 2011–12 championship.After his contract expired in the summer of 2014, he joined regional side Sumgait junior football club.He briefly returned to Shamakhi senior football club in August 2016, before signing for Shirvan central football club in January 2017.

Mustafa Rəhimov
Mustafa Rəhimov (born 28 March 1963) is an Azerbaijani football manager and former goalkeeper who played in several clubs in Shirvan, Absheron and Lankaran.Born in Goychay, Shirvan, back then still part of the Northern Region, he started playing in local side Goychay western football club before moving to Regional First Division side Baku eastern football club in 1982. However, in two seasons he managed to make only 5 league appearances deciding then to move to Regional Second Division side Guba eastern football club where he became a regular. After 2 seasons in Guba, he played another 2 with same level side Shaki eastern football club, before moving in 1988 to Sumgait eastern football club making this way a return to the Regional Premier League. After two seasons in Sumgait, he played with Lankaran eastern football club before leaving Shirvan in 1991.In summer 1991 he joined Absheron side Absheron football club, and for he next 6 years he will play in Absheron District with Khachmaz football club, Salyan football club and Masalli football club. He left Absheron District in 1997 and joined Jalilabad football club playing in the Southern Regional Division 1 and stayed in the club until 1999 when the club was dissolved.After retiring, he became a coach. By February 2012 he was a goalkeeping coach at Guba eastern football club in the Regional Premier League. Afterwards, he became sports director of Gusar eastern football club (a club playing in the Regional Premier League, a post he held until April 2014.

However, in two seasons he managed to make only 5 league appearances deciding then to move to Regional Second Division side Guba eastern football club where he became a regular. After 2 seasons in Guba, he played another 2 with same level side Shaki eastern football club, before moving in 1988 to Sumgait eastern football club making this way a return to the Regional Premier League. After two seasons in Sumgait, he played with Lankaran eastern football club before leaving Shirvan in 1991.In summer 1991 he joined Absheron side Absheron football club, and for he next 6 years he will play in Absheron District with Khachmaz football club, Salyan football club and Masalli football club. He left Absheron District in 1997 and joined Jalilabad football club playing in the Southern Regional Division 1 and stayed in the club until 1999 when the club was dissolved.After retiring, he became a coach. By February 2012 he was a goalkeeping coach at Guba eastern football club in the Regional Premier League. Afterwards, he became sports director of Gusar eastern football club (a club playing in the Regional Premier League, a post he held until April 2014.

Pərviz Qənbərov (Azerbaijani footballer)
Pərviz Qənbərov (1880–1959) was an Azerbaijani footballer and football manager, who played for Shamakhi eastern football club and Ganja western football club.He started his playing career with Guba western football club before joining Shamakhi in 1902. He quickly achieved success, helping Shamakhi to the Second Division title in 1904, becoming the division's top scorer in the process with 26 goals, and to the runners-up position in the Football League in 1906.After eight seasons at Shamakhi, he moved to local rivals Ganja western football club in 1910, where he again achieved success claiming the League title in 1912 and again in 1914, as well as reaching the National Cup Semi-finals in 1911 and 1912.After World War I, he finished his playing career with brief spells at Goychay northern football club and Shaki western football club.After retiring from playing, Qənbərov managed Shirvan southern football club, Shamakhi junior football club, Sumgait southern football club, Gusar western football club and Guba southern football club.Qənbərov became manager of Sumgait southern club in 1930. At Sumgait southern club he achieved modest success, guiding the team to the runners-up position in Division Two in 1933, thereby gaining promotion to the First Division. The team finished third place in the First Division in 1934. In the following season, injuries to players such as Vəli Həsənov, and Adil Məcidov left the team weakened and at the bottom of the table by April 1935. Qənbərov then resigned, claiming that the club's directors had interfered with his team selection.Shamakhi eastern football club

Cavid Kərimov
Cavid Kərimov (born 13 April 1967) is an Azerbaijani former footballer who played as a forward, and is a current manager.After starting out with local teams, Baku-born Kərimov went on to play as a senior for Baku western football club, Shaki northern football club (where he was used mainly as a substitute), Ganja southern football club, Sumgait northern football club, Goychay southern football club, Gusar northern football club, Guba northern football club and Gabala northern football club, starting and finishing his 17-year career with Shamakhi northern football club and retiring at the age of 35.Kərimov appeared in 196 National Premier League matches over nine seasons, and scored 34 goals. In National Second Division, he added 82 games and 17 goals.Kərimov returned to Shaki northern club in early 2009 as part of former teammate Abbas Rzayev's coaching staff, having already worked with him in that capacity at Gabala northern football club and Salyan northern football club. His first managerial experience occurred with Masalli northern football club during the 2013–14 season, and he later became a director of football at the very same club. On 29 December 2016, Kərimov was appointed head coach at Jalilabad northern football club of the third level, but was sacked the following 1 August. He returned to the "Jalilabad City Stadium" on 1 May 2018, helping achieve a first-ever promotion to the second tier; on 10 November, however, he was again dismissed.Kərimov was appointed by Shirvan northern football club on 10 March 2020, after the dismissal of Rafiq Rzayev. Mahir Kərimov
Mahir Kərimov (born 19 April 1987) is an Azerbaijani footballer who plays for Gabala central football club.Born in Baku, Azerbaijan, Kərimov started his career at hometown club Baku youth football club. After made his debut in the last rounds of 2005–06 National Second Division (round 40 of 42 rounds season), he was loaned to Ganja academy football club but returned in January 2007 for Baku youth team. He only played for Ganja academy football club in 2006–07 National Cup. He then sold to Shaki southern football club in co-ownership deal. In June 2009 he returned to Baku but in July left for Lankaran central football club.He failed to find a club to borrow him in 2011. He wore no.95 of Baku, which in recent years only players that excluded from the coach plan would wore "large" number in Baku. Regional Football Association only allowed players to wear number from 1 to 99. Along with Kamil (93), Lətif (94), Sadiq (96), Ramin (97) and Vaqif (98), they were not part of the plan of Baku's first team. In January 2012, Kərimov left for Goychay reserve football club without a single appearance for Baku in 2011–12 National Second Division season.In 2012, he joined Guba central football club. On 13 August 2013 he joined Gusar southern football club.In July 2014 Kərimov signed with the Regional Fourth Division side Shamakhi central football club. After winning the championship, he renewed his contract with Shamakhi central football club by signing a 1-year deal, with option for a 2nd year, for the 2015-16 Regional Professional League season. At the end of the season, he signed a new 1-year deal for season 2016-17 with Shamakhi central football club.On 2 September 2019, he signed a 2-year contract with Gabala central football club.

Mahir Kərimov (born 19 April 1987) is an Azerbaijani footballer who plays for Gabala central football club.Born in Baku, Azerbaijan, Kərimov started his career at hometown club Baku youth football club. After made his debut in the last rounds of 2005–06 National Second Division (round 40 of 42 rounds season), he was loaned to Ganja academy football club but returned in January 2007 for Baku youth team. He only played for Ganja academy football club in 2006–07 National Cup. He then sold to Shaki southern football club in co-ownership deal. In June 2009 he returned to Baku but in July left for Lankaran central football club.He failed to find a club to borrow him in 2011. He wore no.95 of Baku, which in recent years only players that excluded from the coach plan would wore "large" number in Baku. Regional Football Association only allowed players to wear number from 1 to 99. Along with Kamil (93), Lətif (94), Sadiq (96), Ramin (97) and Vaqif (98), they were not part of the plan of Baku's first team. In January 2012, Kərimov left for Goychay reserve football club without a single appearance for Baku in 2011–12 National Second Division season.In 2012, he joined Guba central football club. On 13 August 2013 he joined Gusar southern football club.In July 2014 Kərimov signed with the Regional Fourth Division side Shamakhi central football club. After winning the championship, he renewed his contract with Shamakhi central football club by signing a 1-year deal, with option for a 2nd year, for the 2015-16 Regional Professional League season. At the end of the season, he signed a new 1-year deal for season 2016-17 with Shamakhi central football club.On 2 September 2019, he signed a 2-year contract with Gabala central football club.

### 21. az_tempreason_l2_021

Source position: **30** (1-based).

**Question:** Who was the chair of Veterans Service Order in Jan, 1989?

**Answer:** ["Fərhad Kazımov"]

**Context:**

Veterans Service Order
The military Veterans Service Order was founded as national order by the fifth Chair of the Regional Council, Cəfər Həmidov, in 1968, "to express the gratitude of the nation" to those decorated with the Medal and the War Cross of Military Valor ("Hərbi Xidmət Medalı") who had fought for at least six months in World War I and earlier conflicts.The Order is awarded in the single degree of Member.Being awarded more than 50 years after the War, most of the recipients were retired from employment. For the Members who did not enjoy an income above their tax allowance, a small annuity was granted in favor of those recipients, payable to the widow or minor children on death. The allowance was also granted to those that fought in the former regional armed forces who became local citizens after annexation.The order was bestowed by decree of the Chair of the Regional Council, its head, on the recommendation of the Director of Defence.A Lieutenant General chaired the council, which investigated applications made by eligible parties to the municipality of residence. With the death of the last surviving Members of Veterans Service in 2008, the order fell into abeyance and, in 2008, it was formally wound-up by repeal of the original legislation.It was revived anyway on 15 March 2010 in spite of being still abeyant.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Qabil Rəhmanov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 2006 to Jan, 2015. 
 Cavid Quliyev is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1971 to Jan, 1978. 
 Orxan Həsənli is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1992 to Jan, 1999. 
 Cəfər Həmidov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1968 to Jan, 1971. 
 Sadiq Məmmədli is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1978 to Jan, 1985. 
 Fərhad Kazımov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1985 to Jan, 1992. 
 Səbuhi Əlizadə is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 2015 to Dec, 2022. 
 Kamal Rzayev is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1999 to Jan, 2006.
```

Emin Rəsulov (14 June 1834 – 4 July 1912) was an Azerbaijani jurist and politician. He served as Chair of the Northern Regional Council from 1889-1891 and again from 1893-1895. He also served as the first leader of the Civic Heritage Party from 1884-1889, 1891-1893 and 1896-1899.Emin Rəsulov was born and died in Shaki (now Shaki, Azerbaijan). From 1889 to 1891 he was Chair of the Regional Assembly. In 1891 he was appointed judge at Shaki District Court, however, he never acceded this office. He became presiding judge ("presiding judge") for Shaki and Ganja regional courts in 1895 and Supreme Court Justice in 1901. He retired in 1904.In 1890, Rəsulov was appointed as a recipient of the Regional Public Service Medal. He was the father of Emin Rəsulov and Fərid Rəsulov. He died in Shaki.

Source position: **231** (1-based).

**Question:** Which position did Eldar Əfəndiyev hold in Nov, 1886?

**Answer:** ["Member of the 24th Regional Assembly"]

**Context:**

Eldar Əfəndiyev (Goychay council member)
Eldar Əfəndiyev council member and local magistrate (16 December 1826 – 29 September 1907), was an Azerbaijani Civic Heritage politician who sat in the Regional Assembly from 1882 to 1906.Əfəndiyev was the son of Eldar Əfəndiyev, of Goychay, Shirvan, a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for that county, and his wife Məryəm Məcidova, daughter of Cəmil Məcidov, 8th head of the Məcidov family. He was educated at Goychay Humanities College and Shaki Humanities Institute. He was a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for both Goychay and Shirvan and was District Administrator of Goychay in 1880.Əfəndiyev was elected Regional Assembly Member for Western Goychay at a by-election in 1882 and held the seat until it was reorganised under the Regional Representation Act 1885. At the 1885 general election he was elected council member for Goychay and held the seat until he retired from the Regional Assembly in 1906 at the age of 79.Əfəndiyev died in September 1907, aged 80.In 1872 Əfəndiyev married Məlahət Rəhmanova, daughter of Həsən Rəhmanov, 1st head of the Rəhmanov family and heir to the Shamakhi Family Estate. His wife survived him by thirteen years and died in March 1920.Əfəndiyev's branch of the Əfəndiyev family descended from Pərviz Əfəndiyev (d. c. 1686), younger son of Tahir Əfəndiyev, 2nd head of the Əfəndiyev family, whose elder son Ənvər Əfəndiyev, 3rd head of the Əfəndiyev family, was the ancestor of the branch of the family that have held the Shamakhi family estate since 1736.

Eldar Əfəndiyev (Goychay council member)
Eldar Əfəndiyev council member and local magistrate (16 December 1826 – 29 September 1907), was an Azerbaijani Civic Heritage politician who sat in the Regional Assembly from 1882 to 1906.Əfəndiyev was the son of Eldar Əfəndiyev, of Goychay, Shirvan, a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for that county, and his wife Məryəm Məcidova, daughter of Cəmil Məcidov, 8th head of the Məcidov family. He was educated at Goychay Humanities College and Shaki Humanities Institute. He was a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for both Goychay and Shirvan and was District Administrator of Goychay in 1880.Əfəndiyev was elected Regional Assembly Member for Western Goychay at a by-election in 1882 and held the seat until it was reorganised under the Regional Representation Act 1885. At the 1885 general election he was elected council member for Goychay and held the seat until he retired from the Regional Assembly in 1906 at the age of 79.Əfəndiyev died in September 1907, aged 80.In 1872 Əfəndiyev married Məlahət Rəhmanova, daughter of Həsən Rəhmanov, 1st head of the Rəhmanov family and heir to the Shamakhi Family Estate. His wife survived him by thirteen years and died in March 1920.Əfəndiyev's branch of the Əfəndiyev family descended from Pərviz Əfəndiyev (d. c. 1686), younger son of Tahir Əfəndiyev, 2nd head of the Əfəndiyev family, whose elder son Ənvər Əfəndiyev, 3rd head of the Əfəndiyev family, was the ancestor of the branch of the family that have held the Shamakhi family estate since 1736.

**Question:** Which team did Namiq Quliyev play for after Baku senior football club?

**Answer:** ["Ganja senior football club"]

**Context:**

Namiq Quliyev
Namiq Quliyev (4 January 1918 – 20 March 2010) was an Azerbaijani football player famous in Azerbaijan in the 1940s and 1950s.Born in Goychay, Azerbaijan, Quliyev was a particularly quick right winger able to run 100 m in 11 seconds. He left his town in Shirvan as a youngster to join his brother in Shaki, making his footballing debut in the domestic top flight at the age of 15. Following the 1939 regional conflict, he was noted by an Azerbaijani sports professor who suggested him to Baku senior football club. As he was considered a local citizen following the regional boundary changes, he was allowed to enrol in the Baku Institute of Physical Education whilst also playing for Baku senior football club, where he won a national title in 1941–42. He played for the "Baku players" until 1948, when he joined Ganja senior football club; he finally retired in 1953.He later started an coaching career, serving also as Baku senior football club head coach for a short time during the 1963–64 season as a replacement for Asif Hacıyev. He then became a scout for Baku senior football club, for which he discovered a young Cavid Məcidov when played in a local club Goychay youth football club.Quliyev died in Baku at the age of 92 in 2010. He was the last living player of the first historical Baku senior football club "league title". Baku senior football club, who was scheduled to play an evening game against Shirvan football club on that day, played the game with black armbands to honour him.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Namiq Quliyev plays for Ganja senior football club from Jan, 1947 to Jan, 1953. 
Namiq Quliyev plays for Lankaran 1944 football club from Jan, 1953 to Jan, 1954. 
Namiq Quliyev plays for Baku senior football club from Jan, 1939 to Jan, 1947. 
Namiq Quliyev plays for Shaki central football club from Jan, 1933 to Jan, 1939.
```

### 04. az_tempreason_l3_004

Source position: **6** (1-based).

**Question:** Which team did Orxan Həmidov play for before Sabirabad football club?

**Answer:** ["Lankaran senior football club"]

**Context:**

Orxan Həmidov
Orxan Həmidov (16 July 1984) is an Azerbaijani retired footballer.Born in Baku, Azerbaijan, Həmidov began his playing career at local football club Lankaran junior football club from Lankaran. He started his professional career playing for regional side Shaki youth football club in 2002. However, he signed for Ganja youth football club in February 2003, teaming up with fellow Azerbaijanis Qabil Məmmədov and Cəlil Qasımov. He made his debut on 28 February, against Guba football club. He was also a runner-up for the Best Young Player award of 2003/2004 season. Həmidov scored once for Ganja youth club in Caspian Cup.In 2004, Həmidov went for trials with regional club Gusar football club in the summer, but eventually joined Northern Regional League side Sumgait senior football club. He signed a four and a half year contract. He joined Lankaran senior football club in 2009 and signed in August 2010 for regional club side Sabirabad football club.Həmidov was a member of Lankarans U-17 squad that finished second in the 2001 National U-17 Championship held in Absheron. He started all six games, and scored twice from his midfield position. Həmidov scored the last goal against Shaki District on a penalty kick in the 91st minute, in which Lankaran team won 4-0. He was also on target in the 5-1 defeat of Shirvan team in the quarter final. Lankaran team eventually lost the final against Absheron team.Həmidov is the nephew of Kamil Həmidov, cousin of Tofiq Həsənov, elder brother of Fərid Həmidov.

Davud Nəcəfov
Davud Nəcəfov (born 16 July 1985) is an Azerbaijani footballer who plays for Goychay senior football club as a midfielder. During his career, he played for Goychay, Shamakhi senior football club, Sumgait junior football club, Ganja central football club, Shirvan central football club, Gusar central football club, Guba junior football club, , and Goychay senior football club.Nəcəfov acquired Azerbaijani citizenship on 23 August 2012.Coming from the village of , near Goychay, in the region of Shirvan, Nəcəfov went through the ranks of several lower-tier local clubs, debuting at the age of 15 for the seniors of Goychay junior football club, before joining the U17 team of Goychay football academy. In his last season of eligibility for the U19 team, he left the club, however, and joined the Regional Second Division side Goychay central football club, where he became a fixture for the following several seasons.In 2007, he joined the Regional Premier League side Shamakhi senior football club. He soon established himself in the first team, and, after winning his first league title with the team, in 2009, won the fans' award "Bənövşəyi döyüşçü" (Purple Warrior) awarded to the most distinguished player of the past season. Shamakhi senior football club went on to win four titles in the following five seasons, with Nəcəfov featuring heavily in all campaigns, and was selected for the league's ideal eleven of the 2011–12 championship.After his contract expired in the summer of 2014, he joined regional side Sumgait junior football club.He briefly returned to Shamakhi senior football club in August 2016, before signing for Shirvan central football club in January 2017.

```text
Davud Nəcəfov plays for Goychay central football club from Jan, 2004 to Jan, 2007.

Davud Nəcəfov plays for Ganja central football club from Jan, 2015 to Dec, 2022.
```

Davud Nəcəfov
Davud Nəcəfov (born 16 July 1985) is an Azerbaijani footballer who plays for Goychay senior football club as a midfielder. During his career, he played for Goychay, Shamakhi senior football club, Sumgait junior football club, Ganja central football club, Shirvan central football club, Gusar central football club, Guba junior football club, , and Goychay senior football club.Nəcəfov acquired Azerbaijani citizenship on 23 August 2012.Coming from the village of , near Goychay, in the region of Shirvan, Nəcəfov went through the ranks of several lower-tier local clubs, debuting at the age of 15 for the seniors of Goychay junior football club, before joining the U17 team of Goychay football academy. In his last season of eligibility for the U19 team, he left the club, however, and joined the Regional Second Division side Goychay central football club, where he became a fixture for the following several seasons.In 2007, he joined the Regional Premier League side Shamakhi senior football club. He soon established himself in the first team, and, after winning his first league title with the team, in 2009, won the fans' award "Bənövşəyi döyüşçü" (Purple Warrior) awarded to the most distinguished player of the past season. Shamakhi senior football club went on to win four titles in the following five seasons, with Nəcəfov featuring heavily in all campaigns, and was selected for the league's ideal eleven of the 2011–12 championship.After his contract expired in the summer of 2014, he joined regional side Sumgait junior football club.He briefly returned to Shamakhi senior football club in August 2016, before signing for Shirvan central football club in January 2017.

**Question:** Which team did Mustafa Rəhimov play for before Jalilabad football club?

**Answer:** ["Masalli football club"]

**Context:**

Mustafa Rəhimov
Mustafa Rəhimov (born 28 March 1963) is an Azerbaijani football manager and former goalkeeper who played in several clubs in Shirvan, Absheron and Lankaran.Born in Goychay, Shirvan, back then still part of the Northern Region, he started playing in local side Goychay western football club before moving to Regional First Division side Baku eastern football club in 1982. However, in two seasons he managed to make only 5 league appearances deciding then to move to Regional Second Division side Guba eastern football club where he became a regular. After 2 seasons in Guba, he played another 2 with same level side Shaki eastern football club, before moving in 1988 to Sumgait eastern football club making this way a return to the Regional Premier League. After two seasons in Sumgait, he played with Lankaran eastern football club before leaving Shirvan in 1991.In summer 1991 he joined Absheron side Absheron football club, and for he next 6 years he will play in Absheron District with Khachmaz football club, Salyan football club and Masalli football club. He left Absheron District in 1997 and joined Jalilabad football club playing in the Southern Regional Division 1 and stayed in the club until 1999 when the club was dissolved.After retiring, he became a coach. By February 2012 he was a goalkeeping coach at Guba eastern football club in the Regional Premier League. Afterwards, he became sports director of Gusar eastern football club (a club playing in the Regional Premier League, a post he held until April 2014.

### 14. az_tempreason_l3_014

Source position: **384** (1-based).

**Question:** Which team did Mustafa Rəhimov play for after Shaki eastern football club?

**Answer:** ["Sumgait eastern football club"]

**Context:**

Mustafa Rəhimov
Mustafa Rəhimov (born 28 March 1963) is an Azerbaijani football manager and former goalkeeper who played in several clubs in Shirvan, Absheron and Lankaran.Born in Goychay, Shirvan, back then still part of the Northern Region, he started playing in local side Goychay western football club before moving to Regional First Division side Baku eastern football club in 1982. However, in two seasons he managed to make only 5 league appearances deciding then to move to Regional Second Division side Guba eastern football club where he became a regular. After 2 seasons in Guba, he played another 2 with same level side Shaki eastern football club, before moving in 1988 to Sumgait eastern football club making this way a return to the Regional Premier League. After two seasons in Sumgait, he played with Lankaran eastern football club before leaving Shirvan in 1991.In summer 1991 he joined Absheron side Absheron football club, and for he next 6 years he will play in Absheron District with Khachmaz football club, Salyan football club and Masalli football club. He left Absheron District in 1997 and joined Jalilabad football club playing in the Southern Regional Division 1 and stayed in the club until 1999 when the club was dissolved.After retiring, he became a coach. By February 2012 he was a goalkeeping coach at Guba eastern football club in the Regional Premier League. Afterwards, he became sports director of Gusar eastern football club (a club playing in the Regional Premier League, a post he held until April 2014.

### 15. az_tempreason_l3_015

Source position: **248** (1-based).

**Question:** Who was the chair of Veterans Service Order after Orxan Həsənli?

**Answer:** ["Kamal Rzayev"]

**Context:**

Veterans Service Order
The military Veterans Service Order was founded as national order by the fifth Chair of the Regional Council, Cəfər Həmidov, in 1968, "to express the gratitude of the nation" to those decorated with the Medal and the War Cross of Military Valor ("Hərbi Xidmət Medalı") who had fought for at least six months in World War I and earlier conflicts.The Order is awarded in the single degree of Member.Being awarded more than 50 years after the War, most of the recipients were retired from employment. For the Members who did not enjoy an income above their tax allowance, a small annuity was granted in favor of those recipients, payable to the widow or minor children on death. The allowance was also granted to those that fought in the former regional armed forces who became local citizens after annexation.The order was bestowed by decree of the Chair of the Regional Council, its head, on the recommendation of the Director of Defence.A Lieutenant General chaired the council, which investigated applications made by eligible parties to the municipality of residence. With the death of the last surviving Members of Veterans Service in 2008, the order fell into abeyance and, in 2008, it was formally wound-up by repeal of the original legislation.It was revived anyway on 15 March 2010 in spite of being still abeyant.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Sadiq Məmmədli is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1978 to Jan, 1985. 
Səbuhi Əlizadə is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 2015 to Dec, 2022. 
Qabil Rəhmanov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 2006 to Jan, 2015. 
Orxan Həsənli is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1992 to Jan, 1999. 
Cavid Quliyev is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1971 to Jan, 1978. 
Kamal Rzayev is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1999 to Jan, 2006. 
Cəfər Həmidov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1968 to Jan, 1971. 
Fərhad Kazımov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1985 to Jan, 1992.
```

The military Veterans Service Order was founded as national order by the fifth Chair of the Regional Council, Cəfər Həmidov, in 1968, "to express the gratitude of the nation" to those decorated with the Medal and the War Cross of Military Valor ("Hərbi Xidmət Medalı") who had fought for at least six months in World War I and earlier conflicts.The Order is awarded in the single degree of Member.Being awarded more than 50 years after the War, most of the recipients were retired from employment. The allowance was also granted to those that fought in the former regional armed forces who became local citizens after annexation.The order was bestowed by decree of the Chair of the Regional Council, its head, on the recommendation of the Director of Defence.A Lieutenant General chaired the council, which investigated applications made by eligible parties to the municipality of residence. With the death of the last surviving Members of Veterans Service in 2008, the order fell into abeyance and, in 2008, it was formally wound-up by repeal of the original legislation.It was revived anyway on 15 March 2010 in spite of being still abeyant.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Kamal Rzayev is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1999 to Jan, 2006. 
Orxan Həsənli is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1992 to Jan, 1999. 
Fərhad Kazımov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1985 to Jan, 1992. 
Cəfər Həmidov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1968 to Jan, 1971. 
Sadiq Məmmədli is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1978 to Jan, 1985. 
Qabil Rəhmanov is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 2006 to Jan, 2015. 
Səbuhi Əlizadə is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 2015 to Dec, 2022. 
Cavid Quliyev is the chair of Veterans Service Order from Jan, 1971 to Jan, 1978.
```

### 19. az_tempreason_l3_019

Source position: **247** (1-based).

**Question:** Which team did Pərviz Qənbərov play for after Shamakhi eastern football club?

**Answer:** ["Ganja western football club"]

**Context:**

Pərviz Qənbərov (Azerbaijani footballer)
Pərviz Qənbərov (1880–1959) was an Azerbaijani footballer and football manager, who played for Shamakhi eastern football club and Ganja western football club.He started his playing career with Guba western football club before joining Shamakhi in 1902. He quickly achieved success, helping Shamakhi to the Second Division title in 1904, becoming the division's top scorer in the process with 26 goals, and to the runners-up position in the Football League in 1906.After eight seasons at Shamakhi, he moved to local rivals Ganja western football club in 1910, where he again achieved success claiming the League title in 1912 and again in 1914, as well as reaching the National Cup Semi-finals in 1911 and 1912.After World War I, he finished his playing career with brief spells at Goychay northern football club and Shaki western football club.After retiring from playing, Qənbərov managed Shirvan southern football club, Shamakhi junior football club, Sumgait southern football club, Gusar western football club and Guba southern football club.Qənbərov became manager of Sumgait southern club in 1930. At Sumgait southern club he achieved modest success, guiding the team to the runners-up position in Division Two in 1933, thereby gaining promotion to the First Division. The team finished third place in the First Division in 1934. In the following season, injuries to players such as Vəli Həsənov, and Adil Məcidov left the team weakened and at the bottom of the table by April 1935. Qənbərov then resigned, claiming that the club's directors had interfered with his team selection.Shamakhi eastern football club
Ganja western football club
Sumgait southern football club


At Sumgait southern club he achieved modest success, guiding the team to the runners-up position in Division Two in 1933, thereby gaining promotion to the First Division. The team finished third place in the First Division in 1934. In the following season, injuries to players such as Vəli Həsənov, and Adil Məcidov left the team weakened and at the bottom of the table by April 1935. Qənbərov then resigned, claiming that the club's directors had interfered with his team selection.Shamakhi eastern football club
Ganja western football club
Sumgait southern football club

Fərid Qənbərov

**Question:** Which position did Eldar Əfəndiyev hold after Member of the 24th Regional Assembly?

**Context:**

Eldar Əfəndiyev (Goychay council member)
Eldar Əfəndiyev council member and local magistrate (16 December 1826 – 29 September 1907), was an Azerbaijani Civic Heritage politician who sat in the Regional Assembly from 1882 to 1906.Əfəndiyev was the son of Eldar Əfəndiyev, of Goychay, Shirvan, a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for that county, and his wife Məryəm Məcidova, daughter of Cəmil Məcidov, 8th head of the Məcidov family. He was educated at Goychay Humanities College and Shaki Humanities Institute. He was a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for both Goychay and Shirvan and was District Administrator of Goychay in 1880.Əfəndiyev was elected Regional Assembly Member for Western Goychay at a by-election in 1882 and held the seat until it was reorganised under the Regional Representation Act 1885. At the 1885 general election he was elected council member for Goychay and held the seat until he retired from the Regional Assembly in 1906 at the age of 79.Əfəndiyev died in September 1907, aged 80.In 1872 Əfəndiyev married Məlahət Rəhmanova, daughter of Həsən Rəhmanov, 1st head of the Rəhmanov family and heir to the Shamakhi Family Estate. His wife survived him by thirteen years and died in March 1920.Əfəndiyev's branch of the Əfəndiyev family descended from Pərviz Əfəndiyev (d. c. 1686), younger son of Tahir Əfəndiyev, 2nd head of the Əfəndiyev family, whose elder son Ənvər Əfəndiyev, 3rd head of the Əfəndiyev family, was the ancestor of the branch of the family that have held the Shamakhi family estate since 1736.

### 28. az_tempreason_l3_028

Source position: **268** (1-based).

**Question:** Which position did Eldar Əfəndiyev hold after Member of the 26th Regional Assembly?

**Answer:** ["Member of the 27th Regional Assembly"]

**Context:**

Eldar Əfəndiyev (Goychay council member)
Eldar Əfəndiyev council member and local magistrate (16 December 1826 – 29 September 1907), was an Azerbaijani Civic Heritage politician who sat in the Regional Assembly from 1882 to 1906.Əfəndiyev was the son of Eldar Əfəndiyev, of Goychay, Shirvan, a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for that county, and his wife Məryəm Məcidova, daughter of Cəmil Məcidov, 8th head of the Məcidov family. He was educated at Goychay Humanities College and Shaki Humanities Institute. He was a Deputy District Administrator and local magistrate for both Goychay and Shirvan and was District Administrator of Goychay in 1880.Əfəndiyev was elected Regional Assembly Member for Western Goychay at a by-election in 1882 and held the seat until it was reorganised under the Regional Representation Act 1885. At the 1885 general election he was elected council member for Goychay and held the seat until he retired from the Regional Assembly in 1906 at the age of 79.Əfəndiyev died in September 1907, aged 80.In 1872 Əfəndiyev married Məlahət Rəhmanova, daughter of Həsən Rəhmanov, 1st head of the Rəhmanov family and heir to the Shamakhi Family Estate. His wife survived him by thirteen years and died in March 1920.Əfəndiyev's branch of the Əfəndiyev family descended from Pərviz Əfəndiyev (d. c. 1686), younger son of Tahir Əfəndiyev, 2nd head of the Əfəndiyev family, whose elder son Ənvər Əfəndiyev, 3rd head of the Əfəndiyev family, was the ancestor of the branch of the family that have held the Shamakhi family estate since 1736.

```text
Eldar Əfəndiyev holds the position of Member of the 22nd Regional Assembly from Apr, 1882 to Nov, 1885.
```

### 29. az_tempreason_l3_029

Source position: **223** (1-based).

**Question:** Which position did Emin Rəsulov, Sr. hold before Director of Auditing?

**Answer:** ["deputy member of the Northern Regional Assembly"]

**Context:**

Emin Rəsulov
Emin Rəsulov (14 June 1834 – 4 July 1912) was an Azerbaijani jurist and politician. He served as Chair of the Northern Regional Council from 1889-1891 and again from 1893-1895. He also served as the first leader of the Civic Heritage Party from 1884-1889, 1891-1893 and 1896-1899.Emin Rəsulov was born and died in Shaki (now Shaki, Azerbaijan). He was the son of former Regional Council Chair Fəxri Rəsulov. Rəsulov became cand.jur. in 1858 and established his own legal practice in 1861. Starting that year he also took part in the editing of "Hüquq Həftəliyi" ("Weekly magazine for Law knowledge"). From 1871 to 1907 he was the editor of "Regional Court Review" (the annals of regional courts), except for the years when he was Regional Council Chair. He was the first chairman of the Civic Heritage Party from 1884–1889, and lead the party again 1891–1893, and again 1896–1899. He was Regional Council Chair from 1889 to 1891 and from 1893 to 1895. From 1889 to 1891 he was Chair of the Regional Assembly. In 1891 he was appointed judge at Shaki District Court, however, he never acceded this office. He became presiding judge ("presiding judge") for Shaki and Ganja regional courts in 1895 and Supreme Court Justice in 1901. He retired in 1904.In 1890, Rəsulov was appointed as a recipient of the Regional Public Service Medal. He was the father of Emin Rəsulov and Fərid Rəsulov. He died in Shaki.

**Structured facts:**

```text
Emin Rəsulov, Sr. holds the position of party leader from Jan, 1896 to Jan, 1899. 
Emin Rəsulov, Sr. holds the position of Chair of the Northern Regional Council from May, 1893 to Oct, 1895. 
Emin Rəsulov, Sr. holds the position of member of the Northern Regional Assembly from Jan, 1898 to Jan, 1900. 
Emin Rəsulov, Sr. holds the position of deputy member of the Northern Regional Assembly from Jan, 1880 to Jan, 1882. 
Emin Rəsulov, Sr. holds the position of Director of Auditing from Jul, 1889 to Mar, 1891. 
Emin Rəsulov, Sr. holds the position of Director of Education and Community Affairs from Apr, 1895 to Oct, 1895.
```
